Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a forecasting algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 706. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 197/5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to forecasting theory, we decompose the problem into 706 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 197. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
